# 小实验正式跑第二批

## 任务

用未改动的 `records/051_formal_run.py` 和 `records/051_prompts/` 跑完第一批余下的 19 道，保持 v2、medium、summary=auto、max_output_tokens=32000。先原样存档共有前提补充口径并单独提交；不运行裁判，不解析或比对标准答案及 047 的内容，不报告命中，不判断条数是否足够。047 等已有文件仅按字节计算 SHA256 以保护原文件。
## 结论

第二批执行完 19/19 道的预定流程，共 248 次调用，其中 API 成功 246 次、JSON 解析成功 246 次、截断 0 次。已返回 usage 的第二批估算合计 $13.12883810；29 道合计 $21.79516170。失败尝试无 usage 的部分未知，未加进已知合计；均以账单为准。
第二批 210 次普通叫起中，209 次有可读输出，共取得 730 条前提，其中零条 84 次；29 道 338 次普通叫起中，337 次有可读输出，共取得 1209 条，其中零条 140 次。其余回合未知，不归零。
29 道的程序候选组名键：做法一 50 个、做法二 28 个，按题和原始键计数，名单待 Claude 确认；没有推断组名覆盖哪些表。
**全部统计、token、费用、引用核对来自未脱敏的 `data/051_formal_run_full/` 与 `data/052_formal_run_full/`；公开脱敏后不重算。**
## 详细发现

### 事先存档、范围和执行方式

- 开工 commit：`af9e7af02e7383cd0077d008ba4366c782031e5b`；开工时间 `2026-11-06T12:26:53.336872+00:00`。开工最大编号 051，使用 052；交付前复查无他人占用，未顺延。
- 补充口径单独提交：`cdc85f19fb5dde0f342a106ebd714e1a7aa150b8`，时间 `2026-11-06T20:26:53+08:00`。原文逐字核对并在首条新 API 调用前提交、推送。首条新调用时间 `2026-11-06T12:29:31.554533+00:00`。
- 补充口径：`notes/052_2026-11-06_小实验统计口径补充（共有前提）.md`；核对记录：`records/052_formal_run/frozen_supplement_source_check.json`。
- 新调度脚本 `records/052_formal_run.py` 导入原 051 函数，在内存中只把运行输出根目录改为 052、任务预算改为用户指定的 $30，并修正预算停止标签。保留 F.PUB/F.PROMPTS 指向 051，只读冻结提示词与审计。API 调用、重试、JSON 解析、字节读入、拼装、账本逻辑均由原函数执行；没有改模型参数或任何 047、049、050、051 文件。
- 原 051 脚本 SHA256：`32bb83e7c4f82167829fb01a3cc7b431cf71c7d0269b01c27656564c82fdd268`。重新核对 367 份空账本输入字节哈希及 32 个 Excel 预览块、10,921 个格子，全部通过。
- SDK：OpenAI `3.46.0`；tiktoken `0.14.0`，与 051 相同。
- 19 道范围（沿用 051 未跑名单及其顺序）：impl-006, impl-008, impl-009, impl-010, impl-011, impl-012, impl-017, impl-018, impl-021, impl-023, impl-025, impl-026, impl-030, archeology-hard-1, biomedical-easy-2, biomedical-hard-5, environment-easy-3, environment-hard-16, wildfire-hard-17。
- 预计 248 次调用。预算只累计本任务，排除 051 的费用；停止条件仍为认证失败、模型名被拒、本任务估算超过 $30。
### 统计口径

- 与 051 一致：普通叫起的 target 按完整字符串区分；全部成功条目按 c<叫起>-<条目> 编 id 并追加账本，不因 updates 删旧项，fewer 单独保存。最终清单按模型键计条目出现次数；同一 id 在多个键下出现仍分别计，另列位置，不作语义去重。
- DAComp “agent 写过的表”仍是做法二所用契约节表名的机械近似，模型键去 schema、转小写后完全相同才算出现。空列表键也计出现，同时记录非空比例。这不是组名对表的覆盖判断。
- KramaBench 按 051：单键保留；多键合并按 importance 降序、键序、键内序取前 10，同时保留原始各键条数。
- 新组名口径仅用于列名单：DAComp 最终返回键按 051 的 `rsplit(".",1)[-1].lower()` 规范化，与该题**全部**契约表名比对；不相等的列为候选组名。20 道 DAComp 共 774 个契约表名均为小写且不带 schema；KramaBench 9 道不适用。没有把组条目分配给表、追加到单表清单或调整排名。
- 未取得返回或无法解析的最终清单记为未知，单独列在 unavailable_final_outputs，条数合计只加已有输出；组名无法枚举时 per_task.group_keys 为 null，不当作没有组名。
- 表名出现比例的合计沿用 051 代码，只对具有可用 coverage_proxy 的最终对象加总；存在缺失输出时这是可用子集的描述数，完整范围的比例未知，不能把缺失理解为未出现。是否补跑缺失项交用户和 Claude 决定。
- 第二批计数/引用检查调用原 051 analyze() 及其助手，只适配分析任务选择；新文件中原函数写死的计划题数 10 改记为 19。29 道合计按第一批完整统计与本批完整统计机械加总。所有原始请求和返回保持不变。

  "stg_asana__user": "Only two specific concerns are supported; the required email normalization and validation are implemented.",

  "stg_asana__task": "Five distinct concerns are supported without treating the specified modified_at contamination handling as inherently wrong.",
  "stg_asana__story": "Three concerns are supported, including the cross-model type-contract conflict rather than an assertion that the staging whitelist alone violates its specification.",
  "stg_asana__tag": "The remaining tag transformations follow the unusual but explicit numeric color and notes definitions.",
  "stg_asana__section": "Only three concrete concerns are visible in this staging model.",
  "stg_asana__project_task": "The model preserves relationship rows, casts the declared numeric fields, and filters null required keys as specified; no additional supported concern was found.",
  "stg_asana__task_section": "The model implements the declared types and non-null relationship keys without unsupported deduplication.",
  "stg_asana__task_tag": "The visible transformation matches the supplied relationship contract.",
  "stg_asana__task_follower": "The visible transformation preserves text user identifiers and relationship duplicates while enforcing required keys.",
  "int_asana__task_story": "The local join and ordered case-sensitive classification otherwise match the documented logic.",
  "int_asana__task_assignee": "The assignee flag, exact text join, left-join retention, and duplicate behavior are implemented correctly.",
  "int_asana__task_open_length": "Three supported issues cover elapsed-day semantics, assignment input loss, and the output type.",
  "int_asana__task_comments": "The aggregation delimiter, ordering, null policy, and row count are otherwise implemented as specified.",
  "int_asana__task_first_modifier": "The earliest non-null author selection and optional exact user lookup match the contract, including arbitrary timestamp ties.",
  "int_asana__task_followers": "The left join, alphabetical aggregation, duplicate retention, and joined-row count follow the specification.",
  "int_asana__task_tags": "The implemented text-aligned left join and aggregation match the supplied contract.",
  "int_asana__subtask_parent": "The parent matching, exclusion of unmatched parents, and optional assignee lookup otherwise follow the specification.",
  "int_asana__task_projects": "The source of project_id merits checking; section multiplication, string ordering, and duplicate preservation otherwise match the stated behavior.",
  "int_asana__project_task_metrics": "The three-way inner join, duplicate contributions, conditional counts, and unrounded means match the model specification.",
  "int_asana__project_user": "The final role resolution gives one row per project-user pair with the correct priority; omitting preliminary within-source deduplication does not change that result.",
  "int_asana__user_task_metrics": "The local join, assignee filter, counts, null handling, and grouping follow the contract.",
  "int_asana__task_performance_metrics": "Six distinct concerns are supported; the documented ordered status rules themselves are not treated as an implementation error.",
  "int_asana__user_productivity_trends": "Three issues are visible; the current empty output is consistent with the observed task dates and is not itself evidence of a filtering failure.",
  "int_asana__project_health_indicators": "Three direct issues are supported; other score thresholds and labels largely follow the specification.",
  "asana__user": "The mart's own left join, defaults, and rounding match the supplied specification.",
  "asana__team": "The mart counts project rows, excludes true or null archive flags from active counts, aligns join types, and defaults missing counts as required.",
  "asana__tag": "The mart preserves staging tag rows and counts all task-tag rows using the required exact text-aligned join.",
  "asana__project": "The mart's project retention, user count/list handling, section exclusion, link, and defaults otherwise match its contract.",
  "asana__task": "Three concerns are supported; the required open-length inner join and optional-model default policies are otherwise implemented.",
  "asana__daily_metrics": "The revised query correctly preserves creation/completion-only days, null-day matching, counts, and net differences.",
  "asana__user_performance_dashboard": "Three linked but distinct omitted-baseline effects are supported; current data has no current-month trends, limiting their immediate observed impact.",
  "asana__project_analytics": "Four concrete concerns were found; the remaining scoring, ranking, and validation logic mostly follows the contract.",
  "asana__team_efficiency_metrics": null,
  "asana__task_lifecycle_analysis": "Six direct or material upstream concerns are supported; the membership grain, context defaults, pattern ordering, and final validity filtering are otherwise implemented.",
  "result.md": "Only two specific report-level concerns are supported; this does not assess the final response as a whole."
}

### 引用核对和格式检查

## 证据来源

- 原执行代码及提示词：`records/051_formal_run.py`、`records/051_prompts/`；原输入与拼装：`records/049_small_exp_inputs/`、`records/049_small_exp_assemble.py`。
- 本批调度与分析：`records/052_formal_run.py`；配置 `runtime_configuration.json`、审计 `verification.json`、合并统计 `combined_29/`、组名 `group_keys_method1_29.json/.csv` 与 `group_keys_method2_29.json/.csv`。
- 准备、调用和分析均用 `/tmp/assumption-ledger-050-venv/bin/python`，tokenizer cache `/tmp/assumption-ledger-048-tiktoken-cache`；SDK 版本见 `environment.json`。
```bash
python records/052_formal_run.py prepare
python records/052_formal_run.py run
python records/052_formal_run.py analyze
python records/052_formal_run.py publish
python records/052_formal_run.py verify
python records/052_formal_run.py report
```
## 不确定或没查到的

- 候选组名名单交 Claude 确认，未推断任何组名覆盖的表，未构建裁判附加清单或改变原排名。多点号字符串的规范化仍按 051 的字面规则，相关原文已列。
- 复合 target、别名、同一 id 的跨键重复及字段/格式问题均保留原样，不作语义归并。
- 引用的纯字符串检查不能确认语义支持；无内部引号按指定口径记“未给出原文”。费用未核账，失败尝试无 usage 的部分未知。
## 建议的下一步

请用户和 Claude 确认候选组名；前提句复核完成后再安排裁判与抽查。本任务止于被测模型调用和机械统计。
